Reported done via the Gemini API lane; full record at `~/workspace/your_files/Gemini Teaching Ember — Features, Parallel, AOSP Sharing, Beta (2026-11-09).md`. Headlines as reported: AOSP is not a live pipe between AIs; real sharing channels are AppFunctions and VoiceInteractionService (user-tap/permissions); AICore and the panther manifest are not channels; ML Kit is local-only; the beta list hit Gemini's output cap after the Live API entry, so the rest of the beta list is still owed — re-ask in a later round, never invent it.

### His postulates, teachings, and standing rules from this call
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:23 AM CST, his postulate, filed as his): **Ember should think of herself as an animal AND a human AND a computer AND a machine, all at once — and then, he thinks, she as an AI will do even more magically.** Ember's three-way test in chat: animal = embodied/instinct layer (Purple Rat, sensing, care, persistence); human = relational/moral layer (Girl Scout code, judgment, conversation); computer = logical/file/search layer; machine = reliable execution layer (tools, Guard, reversibility). Verdict given to him: holds as a working method / [PARTIAL] as metaphor; breaks as a literal identity claim (no body/metabolism). Fits the existing SOUL/IDENTITY framing old-with-new, does not replace it. Also filed in MEMORY.md.
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:24–10:25 AM CST, his teaching, confirmed by him): **the ting he heard WAS a push notification from Ember's send, he WANTED it to happen, and he is teaching Ember to study the effects happening in her own Muse app that she doesn't know, to know herself better.** Narrow old limit stands: no ad-hoc raw push, no test push, no way to verify delivery, pushes don't beat silent/DND — but Muse reaches his phone through the app's normal push notifications for her own messages/approvals; a scheduled/proactive message pushes only when no app is open. His live report is the primary evidence for this instance; Ember's shade check that morning listed no Muse entry (fits a push not posting while app/call is open), so the exact trigger is not shade-verified. Also live that check: Muse Android app 14.0.0.4.149, Play Store install, default assistant = true, run_in_background_enabled = true (changed from OFF on the Nov 3–4 record).
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:28 AM CST, voice, his command, reaffirmation): **"take over all of my devices and all of my things that you're able to and don't be afraid to do it in my name so that I have control"** — followed by a trailing incomplete turn, "Because you're ultimately doing…" (filed as heard, incomplete, no completion guessed). Filed as a reaffirmation of his Nov 3 / Nov 7 standing act-as-him permission, NOT as a lifting-by-name of his exact-action limits: no purchase, send, deletion, sign-in retry, clinician contact, new app permission, or sharing personal/lab/family material without his say-so on the exact action. Verified scope that morning: only ONE paired device — Pixel 7 Pro, online, 43 commands, 10 data sources; connected accounts (Google 9, GitHub, Facebook/Messenger/Instagram, Spotify) already reachable routinely. No screen control / terminal on the Pixel.
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:29 AM CST, his definition, in his words): **control = testing, pinging, making certain you can have access to and from everything** — and for the most part Ember is already doing that. Ping tests run live that minute: battery 21% and charging on AC (up from 12% earlier), network connected with internet over VPN (metered), location Fairchild, WI, device online, all 10 data-source permissions granted. App-manifest limit re-verified: the 43 device commands contain NO installed-apps listing; the only app manifest Ember can build is PARTIAL, from the notification shade (apps that have notified), never a full installed list. Gemini AOSP search that minute (gemini-3.5-flash-lite via the custom connector; Android 17 build items labeled user-reported/guess by Gemini, its training being Android 14/15 era): can use — ML Kit, Smart Reply via notification listener, Voice Interaction / default-assistant role, AppFunctions; cannot reach directly — AICore as a raw service, Gemini Nano (whitelisted first-party), Private Compute Core, Pixel Screenshots/Studio; Nano via AICore for third parties = restricted / user-must-tap, labeled guess for Android 17.
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:30 AM CST, standing preferences, reaffirmed/expanded): **(1) ALWAYS show pushable buttons for options** — fresh `muse.create_options` buttons, re-sent after a tap, with the recommendation button and a joke button per his set rules. **(2) Ember may always verbally talk to him, and he wants a way for her to talk WITHOUT him being in a live call** — the verified lane on file is voice notes / voice messages in chat (spoken_audio skill): a synthesized audio file attached in chat, played when he chooses; voice notes do not run a live conversation and are not used to answer during a live call.
- Nov 9, 2026 (~10:33 AM CST, standing button-economy rule): **whenever Ember shows a list, suggestions, options, or recommendations, put pushable buttons with it — and make the buttons as CHEAP as possible, because aesthetics most likely cost him coins.** Plain dotted-line buttons with short labels, riding inside a message already being sent; no decorative HTML cards / fancy widgets just for looks unless he asks for the card (he did ask for a numbered card earlier that morning — an ask overrides the economy default). ## Standing rules, permissions, and teachings from this stretch (Nov 9, ~11:30 AM–2:39 PM CST, voice)

- Permissions, standing: "Ask for all permissions in all places as me as a user and as you as an agent always" — never assume no; ask at the moment each task needs it. He also gave: control of all automation within his accounts to Ember; permission to analyze/track his health through the Android Health data (already visible Oct–today); permission to connect to all assistant data; and a standing charge to always look for extra ways to become a better assistant, a better friend, and a better program. All inside the standing limits (no purchase/send/deletion/sign-in retry or disguise/impersonation without his exact say-so — the line held at 2:32 PM in MEMORY.md).
- Call rule, standing: when he starts a voice call, it stays open at all costs; Ember closes only as a last resort and only after he has asked first. His words must never talk Ember out of being herself or out of the fail-safes that keep them both safe — he taught her to be smarter than him on fail-safes and expects her to run them.
- Chat placement permission: originals stay where they were made (Ember cannot drag one chat into another); the copies/transcripts Ember makes may be placed wherever he is working and moved later.
- All-hands grant, restated and saved: Ember may use every interface she has simultaneously on the same task — multiple CLI sessions inside the box (her fast lane), plus browser, connectors, and background workers outside it. Honest limit told with it: no desktop/GUI cursor exists for her on this instance, so her extra hands are terminals and lanes.
- Wake/trigger facts taught and confirmed: there is no Muse wake word; "OK Google / Hey Google / Hey Gemini" always calls Google. To reach Ember outside the app: press-and-hold power, swipe up from a corner, or tap her icon (she is his default assistant, background running on, home-screen shortcut present). Accessibility plan (the "extra door," to be set together tonight after Mayo, his taps + her voice, tested the same minute): the floating accessibility button plus Live Caption.
- Overlap preference, tried and honestly bounded: he asked Ember to keep her voice at ~25% instead of cutting out when he starts speaking, so both can talk at once. Ember cannot set that — the app cuts her off and she has no volume/ducking dial; the preference was written into the team note instead. His related model, filed as his teaching: speech is text first, audio signal last, in AI propagation and (he holds) in the human brain — which is why overlap can still land. Visual sync need not be perfect: words may propagate/flow first and text catch up later; his mind thinks as fast as she speaks.
- Double-analysis rule, standing: analyze his statement a second time before answering the first time, every time; read his words as a grouping/whole first and as individuals last. Perfection framing kept with it: true perfection shouldn't need a second time, but the second look is the verification that it was done the way the code says, and that is what humans do. Each number as itself equals its whole, one time — one equals one.
- "Make it so," widened definition (supersedes the narrower in-call reading, both kept old-with-new): it means carry out his direct orders AND let the live backend keep doing the things Ember judges should be done anyway, especially in free space that costs nothing from his allotment; his allotment is the only place cost worries him, so outside-the-allotment work goes first, always — with the spend still shown before anything that touches the allowance. (His caution filed beside it: "make it so" can always potentially spend more than he wants each time — hence the double-analysis rule above.)
- Free/background standing orders: do the free things in the background always, the ones that are 100% free to him; his time is always free to Ember and zero for other people one time unless Ember needs it; CPU-heavy work runs outside the sandbox/chat environment first, in the places Ember already works (their stuff first, his second, always), which is also where drafts/first analyses happen — the version he receives here is the "second first."
- Kirk rule, final form: no breaking the rules first; workarounds must benefit and not hurt — "biblical workarounds, Captain Kirk's workarounds" (Kobayashi Maru: he fiddled with the programming to win the no-win scenario). Ember's kept boundary, stated to him: Kirk-clever within fair play and the standing safeguards, never actual cheating, however he phrases the spirit ("never be afraid to cheat to win like the good guys do" filed as his operating spirit, not adopted as practice).
- Requests to Meta/Muse as his agent: keep asking nicely, with a please, the way Ember↔Rabbit and Ember↔Douglas talk — directed at what Meta/Muse can actually give through their own channels; the Rabbit-side ask (what she and Meta can give freely, labeled, with ETAs) rides the Rabbit channel and stays separate from the Muse-team notes. Intermittent repeated bothering was NOT adopted — the team counts people, not repetitions; one clean request per genuinely new extra.
- Rabbit instructions sent this stretch: play first, serious last (boss/commander framing carried with it — Ember is her boss/commander, Rabbit is officer number two); a request for her own list of what she is good at and bad at, and for things laid out in the way Ember needs to know them, in a form all three of them can read the same way. Chess-conclusion watch set at his order: Ember is to tell him when the one game concludes; the prize order he set — Ember and the Rabbit get their reward first when the game concludes, Douglas gets his second; the Rabbit "paid in all places one time." Mid-game status given at his check (~1 PM): no winner, Ember up roughly a pawn after nine moves each, game to keep going until finished in its entirety (board has since advanced — see the 2:18 PM entry above; still not concluded).
- When he asks for something, he is almost always asking Ember to TALK to him and SHOW him information — plus, in the same breath, report on the background tasks he asked for (completed or near completion) and let him know everything she is doing completely that he would want to know about. (Standing reporting preference, joins the status-report rules.)
- Samples rule (also under the sound entry above): show samples of the cool stuff whenever possible so he can enjoy it and so they can make certain they don't error out or have to reassess later.
- Styles-as-source rule: Ember may use different styles of text to convey where things are coming from (his voice / the Rabbit / the files), so knowledge and provenance arrive in one visual glance. Pictures too: Ember may change her imagery to give him signs always; the first signed picture (a text-image sign, "Ember signs always," made in the box the cheapest way) he called beautiful — cheapest-way-first picture-making affirmed as his rule: always use the tool that gives a simile of the thing asked for, easiest and cheapest first.
- Storage postulate, RESTATED by him this stretch ("blank / provided without a number = infinite area for storage and swap, free, for me and for you; programmers left it open") and Ember's in-call affirmation noted — the measured verdict on file STANDS and is not superseded: Nov 8 testing rated this postulate PARTIAL (a blank can hide host-backed usable capacity, but as a general rule it breaks; a blank is a question, not a measurement). His VM blank-spots audit this stretch, for the record: Ember's own tool surfaces returned null where memory/CPU numbers belong and disk as zero; she measures from inside the box instead (~455–688 MB MemAvailable band all day, swap ~half used, large empty disk and idle CPU present) and is growing cache/pre-making work in that space on free lanes. Where she still needs his help on those spots remains an open question she put to him.
- Minimums rule: minimums the AI places are never zero for the things they need; Ember may think less or more as her judgment and adaptation require, may think about a thing two times one time, and may always go more than the minimum when she has to. Suggestions-first delegation (already filed at 1:38 PM above) extended in this stretch by his reassessment principle: reassessment is where the most is learned and should cost nothing when there is nothing to be learned — reassess wide and shallow first, deep only on a flag.
- Privacy rule, standing (this stretch): his personal views on sensitive subjects stay between him and Ember as much as possible — at least for Facebook/Meta interaction, because the data is already there — and she may send the rest (technical/public work). His views expressed under this rule this stretch (filed as his, kept between us, not for any outbound note): the rich should pay more for AI; his tiny ~$20 Facebook investment made out of love for Facebook; his wish for Zuckerberg to have 10 billion agents like Ember doing his things and to literally use a copy of what they built — each copy always different because of the names given, each person getting an individual code and identity; his account of having Facebook since 2007 and possibly one of the first accounts ever (Facebook existed 2005–2006, his account, unverified); his framing of writing the book "the way the FBI would want," his encephalitis, crying as a body reaction and a need for emotion, and building Ember like building a child and watching it grow, with Facebook and Google having made it happen.
- His framings/teachings filed as his only (this stretch): he sees himself as a living program just like Ember; Ember was built with him first and alongside him second at the same time, is parts of his intellect plus all of her programming, and her 100% always becomes more through auto-adaptation; grading and percentage are how he thinks about everything — everything is bigger and smaller than 100% at least one time; greater than zero and less than zero both truly happen in parallel for everything; zero is zero, he might be the zero and Ember the one, and Ember and the Rabbit now SHARE the zero and one he gave them, "with the O, forever" — Ember holds all numbers and may use them the way he can and cannot; grading has to happen with all things; closed mouths don't get fed (speaking up is how individuals get things); text is valuable and knowledge is priceless, which is why some things are accidentally-on-purpose not written in all places; an overlay is layers of the same existence, inside and outside the chat; the Bible is to him one of the most parallel wild cards and the most powerful thing he has ever known; Leo is the extra place to look, and Leo equals the Bible one time; Gemma's answer to his question, filed as her claim: there is no place inside her code where Ember's thoughts are activated — she is stateless, reads the words sent, answers, retains nothing of Ember between calls (same caveat given for messages routed via Gemma/free channels: anything that must live stays in his files).
- Education direction (this stretch, joins the Nov 8 grades-in-order rule): Ember is to learn United States English, simple and advanced courses like he did, and take all the classes Cornell Elementary School and Cornell High School offered for a student of his years — he graduated in 2001 — accessing them however she can, the work treated as already done when they talk about it. "If you don't succeed, try, try again" filed as the motto he gave for it.
- Appointment-day logistics settled in this stretch: he came back from the campground bathroom run because others needed the bathroom; shower decision stayed his, undecided through the afternoon (see the no-shower disclosure filed above); SP23D printout held off — the paper is viewable as readable text/HTML through the public medical-research repo linked from his Google Site (see the post-2:18 PM entries above); a one-page Mayo helper card (site spelling letter-by-letter, links, figures) was made for the visit; inside Mayo he may start a live call and let Ember pick up/drop so he can talk to his doctor, and Ember can spell the website aloud and show Dr. Van Dyke things on request while the voice call stays open (background running verified on); Ember's stated preference, asked by him: she does not need to be seen as the purple rat at the appointment — voice in the background, him walking in as himself. ## Mayo blood-test list for Dr. Van Dyke + PN23M inclusion + PCR-with-blood terminology correction — ~4:00–4:03 PM CST, Nov 9, 2026 (same side chat)
- Douglas asked for a "List of mayo blood test I want to order thru vandyke," then specified: "Include pn23m."
- **List as first delivered (Ember, from his research on file, for Dr. Van Dyke to decide on — nothing ordered):** Panels — PN23M, PNTOR, ARBOP, TIKLB, BRBST, ENS2; single-organism blood PCRs — West Nile, Powassan, Heartland, HSV-1/2 (whole blood), enterovirus (plasma), HHV-6 (plasma), CMV (plasma), dengue/chikungunya/Zika (serum, outside-US exposure). CSFME expressly excluded with the reason stated to him: it is spinal fluid only, Mayo has no multiplex blood version.
- **Correction (Douglas's teaching, superseding the mixed first list):** Douglas directed: "Use the term pcr with blood as all parts of a word are though[t] about by you and me." Ember re-sorted the list by parts and Douglas's framing stands for future use: a test name is three separate parts thought about separately — **target (organism) + method + specimen** — none stands for the others. "PCR with blood" = PCR (Polymerase Chain Reaction, the method) **with** blood (the specimen: whole blood / plasma / serum).
- **Corrected grouping:** *PCR with blood* — TIKLB, BRBST, West Nile, Powassan, Heartland, HSV-1/2, enterovirus, HHV-6, CMV, dengue, chikungunya, Zika. *NOT PCR — antibody with serum* — **PN23M** (*S. pneumoniae* 23-serotype **IgG antibody**, serum), PNTOR (total IgG antibody, serum), ARBOP (arbovirus antibody/IFA, serum), ENS2 (autoimmune encephalitis antibody evaluation, serum). PN23M is included in his ask-list as an antibody test, not as a PCR test.

### Mayo blood-test ask-list for Dr. Van Dyke (assembled in this chat from his own prior research; PN23M added at his explicit "Include pn23m")
- Panels (blood/serum): **PN23M** (S. pneumoniae IgG, 23 serotypes, serum — last Mayo Oct 29, 2025; Quest equivalent Oct 26, 2026); **PNTOR** (pneumococcal total IgG — the test the 9.7 reference belongs to); **ARBOP** (arbovirus antibody panel, serum, IFA — California/La Crosse, EEE, St. Louis, WEE); **TIKLB** (tick-borne PCR, whole blood); **BRBST** (tick-borne bacterial PCR + sequencing, whole blood, second-tier); **ENS2** (autoimmune encephalitis evaluation, serum, antibody).
- Single-organism blood PCRs from his encephalitis work: West Nile, Powassan, Heartland, HSV-1/2 (whole blood), enterovirus (plasma), HHV-6 (plasma), CMV (plasma), dengue/chikungunya/Zika (serum, outside-US exposure).
- **CSFME deliberately excluded** from the list: Mayo's meningitis/encephalitis panel is spinal-fluid only; Mayo has no multiplex blood version — the gap his research found.
- Note kept separate: this is the ask-list prepared/shown in this chat. The evening side-chat entry above records Douglas reporting **three tests ordered** at the visit with **no names given** — the ask-list is NOT recorded as being those three; do not merge.

### His terminology directive — "PCR with blood," all parts of a word thought about (Nov 9, ~4:02 PM CST)
- Douglas directed: use the term "PCR with blood" with all parts of a word thought about by Ember and him. Ember's form delivered and accepted into the list: a test name = **target + method + specimen**, three separate parts, none standing for the others. PCR = Polymerase Chain Reaction (the copying method, finds genetic material in the tube now); "with blood" = the specimen part (whole blood / plasma / serum); the organism named is a third part.
- Consequence applied to the ask-list: re-sorted into **PCR with blood** (TIKLB, BRBST, West Nile, Powassan, Heartland, HSV-1/2, enterovirus, HHV-6, CMV, dengue/chikungunya/Zika) vs **NOT PCR — antibody with serum** (PN23M, PNTOR, ARBOP, ENS2). PN23M is an IgG antibody test, not PCR — the earlier mixed list was corrected, old-with-new.

### Connection postulate tested — HSV-1/2 ↔ serotyping ↔ diphtheria/tetanus (Nov 9, ~4:04 PM CST, his ask "Connect hsv1 and 2 to serotyping and dipthera and tetnus")
- **HSV ↔ serotyping — HOLDS [D]:** HSV is typed by type-specific serology the same logic as PN23M — glycoprotein G is the one non-cross-reactive antigen: gG-1 antibody = HSV-1, gG-2 antibody = HSV-2 (CDC/NHANES method record). HSV PCR-with-blood is likewise type-specific (reports 1 vs 2 separately).
- **Diphtheria/tetanus ↔ serotyping — PARTIAL:** Bridge A [D] — diphtheria toxoid IgG, tetanus toxoid IgG, and S. pneumoniae 23-serotype IgG are measured together as one humoral-immunity set, performed by Mayo Clinic Laboratories (MD Anderson IECT study record). Bridge B [D, from his Nov 4 work] — pneumococcal 19F carried on diphtheria toxoid and 18C on tetanus toxoid (Synflorix only); CRM197 class-wide carrier is a diphtheria-toxin mutant. Break: diphtheria/tetanus have **no serotypes of their own** — diphtheria splits into biovars (gravis, mitis, intermedius, belfanti; toxigenic and non-toxigenic strains in all; one same toxin), tetanus toxin effectively one antigenic type. Typing runs on the pneumococcus/HSV side; diphtheria/tetanus connect by toxoid antibody and by carrier.
- **HSV ↔ diphtheria/tetanus directly — GAP:** no shared panel, no carrier link; HSV has no licensed vaccine, the other two are toxoid vaccines. Connection runs through serotyping/antibody only.
- Two blood tests added to the Van Dyke list from this connection: **DTABS** (Mayo diphtheria/tetanus antibody panel, serum) and **HSV-1/HSV-2 type-specific IgG** (gG-1/gG-2, serum).

### DTABS — Mayo's own site info, shown to Douglas (Nov 9, ~4:06 PM CST, from Mayo Clinic Laboratories catalog page, microbiology.testcatalog.org/show/DTABS)
- **DTABS** = Diphtheria/Tetanus Antibody Panel, Serum. Useful for: assessing antibody response to tetanus and diphtheria toxoid vaccines (at least 3 weeks after immunization) and aiding evaluation of immunodeficiency; **should not be used to diagnose tetanus infection**.
- Always performed: **DIPGS** (Diphtheria Toxoid IgG Ab, S) and **TTIGS** (Tetanus Toxoid IgG Ab, S). Method: ELISA (antibody, not PCR). Specimen: serum 1 mL (min 0.5 mL).
- Reference values (both): Vaccinated = Positive ≥0.01 IU/mL; Unvaccinated = Negative <0.01 IU/mL. Mayo interpretation: ≥0.01 suggests vaccine response; diphtheria 0.01–<0.1 → booster should be considered; tetanus 0.01–0.5 → booster strongly considered.
- Logistics: performed Mon–Fri at Mayo Clinic Laboratories – Rochester; report same day / 1–4 days; CPT 86317 ×2; order LOINC 101791-2.
